Fight Night: Dern vs. Rodriguez]]\n}}\n\n'''''UFC Fight Night: Santos vs. Walker''''' (also known as '''''UFC Fight Night 193''''', '''''UFC Vegas 38''''' and '''''UFC on ESPN+ 51''''') was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that took place on October 2, 2021 at the [[UFC Apex]] facility in [[Enterprise, Nevada]], part of the [[Las Vegas–Paradise, NV MSA|Las Vegas Metropolitan Area]], United States.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/lists/ufc-events-approved-by-nevada-state-athletic-commission-october-november-las-vegas|title=NAC approves six UFC events stretching from October through November|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2021-09-23|access-date=2021-09-24}}\n\n==Background==\nA light heavyweight bout between former [[UFC Light Heavyweight Championship]] title challenger [[Thiago Santos]] and [[Johnny Walker (fighter)|Johnny Walker]] headlined the event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2021/6/15/22535243/thiago-santos-johnny-walker-pushed-back-week-headlines-oct-2-ufc-event|title=Thiago Santos vs. Johnny Walker pushed back a week, now headlines Oct. 2 UFC event|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Guilherme Cruz|date=2021-06-15|access-date=2021-06-15}}\n\nA women's bantamweight bout between [[Aspen Ladd]] and [[The Ultimate Fighter: Heavy Hitters]] featherweight winner [[Macy Chiasson]] was expected to take place at the event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.espn.com.co/mma/nota/_/id/8975921/combate-aspen-ladd-macy-chiasson-nueva-fecha-ufc|title=The fight between Aspen Ladd and Macy Chiasson has a new date|publisher=espn.com.co|author=Carlos Contreras Legaspi|date=2021-07-29|access-date=2021-07-29}} {{in lang|es}} The pairing was initially scheduled for [[UFC on ESPN: Sandhagen vs. Dillashaw]], but it was removed from that card on July 19 after Chiasson suffered a [[stress fracture]] in her foot.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2021/7/24/22591261/macy-chiasson-says-ufc-vegas-32-withdrawal-was-due-to-stress-fracture-in-foot|title=Macy Chiasson says UFC Vegas 32 withdrawal was due to stress fracture in foot|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Alexander K. Lee|date=2021-07-24|access-date=2021-07-24}} At the weigh-ins, Ladd weighed in at 137 pounds, one pound over the bantamweight non-title limit, leading to the fight cancellation.{{Cite web|date=2021-10-01|title=Aspen Ladd's troubling weigh-in leads to UFC Fight Night 193 bout cancellation|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/10/ufc-fight-night-193-weigh-in-video-aspen-ladd-overweight-troubling-drawn-trip-scale|access-date=2021-10-02|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday|author=Mike Bohn and Ken Hathaway}}\n\nA light heavyweight bout between [[Jimmy Crute]] and [[Jamahal Hill]] was initially targeted for the event.{{Cite web|title=Jimmy Crute vs. Jamahal Hill booked for UFC Fight Night on Oct. 2|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/07/ufc-news-jimmy-crute-vs-jamahal-hill-booked-light-heavyweight-october|date=2021-07-09|access-date=2021-07-10|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Mike Bohn and Nolan King}} However, the bout was rescheduled in early September for [[UFC Fight Night: Font vs. Aldo]] two months later.{{Cite web|date=2021-09-06|author=Shaun Al-Shatti|title=Jimmy Crute vs. Jamahal Hill targeted for UFC event on December 4|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2021/9/6/22659924/jimmy-crute-vs-jamahal-hill-targeted-for-ufc-event-on-december-4|access-date=2021-09-07|publisher=mmafighting.com}}\n\nA lightweight bout between [[Carlos Diego Ferreira]] and [[Grant Dawson]] was scheduled for the event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmadna.nl/carlos-diego-ferreira-vs-grant-dawson-toegevoegd-aan-ufc-evenement-op-2-oktober/|title=Carlos Diego Ferreira vs. Grant Dawson added to UFC event on October web|date=2021-09-08|author=Matthew Wells|title=Diego Ferreira injured, out of Oct. 2 bout against Grant Dawson|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/09/ufc-news-diego-ferriera-out-of-oct-2-bout-against-grant-dawson-due-to-injury|access-date=2021-09-08|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com}} Dawson was then rescheduled to face [[Ricky Glenn]] at [[UFC Fight Night: Costa vs. Vettori]].{{Cite web|url=https://mmadna.nl/breaking-ricky-glenn-vervangt-carlos-diego-ferreira-op-23-oktober-tegen-grant-dawson/|title=BREAKING: Ricky Glenn replaces Carlos Diego Ferreira on October 23 against Grant Santos]] and [[Alexander Hernandez]] were expected to meet in a lightweight bout at the event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2021/8/11/22620233/alexander-hernandez-vs-leonardo-santos-set-for-oct-2-ufc-event|title=Alexander Hernandez vs. Leonardo Santos set for Oct. 2 UFC event|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Mike Heck|date=2021-08-11|access-date=2021-08-11}} However, Santos pulled out of the event due to undisclosed reasons.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/09/ufc-fight-night-193-news-leonardo-santos-out-vs-alexander-hernandez|title=Leonardo Santos out of UFC Fight Night 193 bout vs. Alexander Hernandez|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2021-09-23|access-date=2021-09-23}} He was replaced by Mike Breeden.{{Cite web|url=https://mmadna.nl/breaking-mike-breeden-vervangt-leonardo-santos-tegen-alexander-hernandez-op-2-oktober/|title=Mike Breeden replaces Leonardo Santos against Alexander Hernandez on October 2|publisher=mmadna.nl|author=Marcel Dorff|date=2021-09-24|access-date=2021-09-27}} {{in lang|nl}}\n\nAt the weigh-ins, two other fighters missed weight for their respective bouts. Mike Breeden weighed in at 158.5 pounds, two and a half pounds over the lightweight non-title fight limit. Former [[UFC Women's Bantamweight Championship]] challenger [[Bethe Correia]] weighed in at 138.5 pounds, two and a half pounds over the women's bantamweight non-title fight limit. Both bouts proceeded at a catchweight with Breeden and Correia each fined 20% of their purses, which went to their opponents [[Alexander Hernandez]] and [[Karol Rosa]] respectively.{{Cite arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that took place on October 9, 2021 at the [[UFC Apex]] facility in [[Enterprise, Nevada]], part of the [[Las Vegas–Paradise, NV MSA|Las Vegas Metropolitan Area]], United States.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/lists/ufc-events-approved-by-nevada-state-athletic-commission-october-november-las-vegas|title=NAC approves six [[Marina Rodriguez]] and [[Mackenzie Dern]] headlined the event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/07/ufc-news-marina-rodriguez-mackenzie-dern-main-event-fight-night-october-strawweight|title=Marina Rodriguez vs. Mackenzie Dern headlines UFC Fight in October|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Mike Bohn|date=2021-07-09|access-date=2021-07-09}}\n\nA middleweight bout between [[Phil Hawes]] and [[Deron Winn]] was expected to take place at the event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.sherdog.com/news/news/Phil-Hawes-vs-Deron-Winn-Rescheduled-for-UFC-Card-on-Oct-9-180512|title=Phil Hawes vs. Deron Winn rescheduled for UFC card on Oct. 9|date=2021-07-15|access-date=2021-07-15|author=Tristen Critchfield|publisher=sherdog.com}} The pairing was initially scheduled to meet on July 17 at [[UFC on ESPN: Makhachev vs. Moisés]], but Winn pulled out of that event citing a chest injury.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2021/7/5/22564574/deron-winn-out-of-phil-hawes-fight-at-ufc-vegas-31-due-to-injury|title=Deron Winn out of Phil Hawes fight at UFC Vegas 31 due to injury|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Alexander K. Lee|date=2021-07-05|access-date=2021-07-15}} In turn, Winn withdrew the day before the event due to health issues.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/10/chris-curtis-short-notice-vs-phil-hawes-ufc-fight-night-194-deron-winn-out|title= With Deron Winn out, Chris Curtis gets short-notice shot vs. Phil Hawes at UFC Fight Night 194|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Staff|date=2021-10-08|access-date=2021-10-08}} He was briefly replaced by [[Chris Curtis (fighter)|Chris Curtis]], but Hawes declined the bout and it was ultimately scrapped.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2021/10/8/22716518/phil-hawes-vs-chris-curtis-short-notice-bout-off-of-ufc-vegas-39 |title=Phil Hawes vs. Chris Curtis short-notice bout off of UFC Vegas 39|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Alexander K. web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/08/mike-trizano-vs-chas-skelly-ufc-fight-night-october-9-mma-matchup-booking|title=Mike Trizano vs. Chas Skelly added to UFC Fight Night on Oct. [[UFC Fight Night: Costa vs. Vettori]] after one of Pickett's coaches [[COVID-19 testing|tested positive for COVID-19]].{{Cite web|date=2021-10-05|title=Jamie Pickett out of UFC Fight Night 194, bout vs. Laureano Staropoli rescheduled for Oct. 9|access-date=2021-10-06|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Mike Heck}} However, Hughes was pulled from the bout after testing positive for [[COVID-19]] and was replaced by promotional newcomer [[Silvana Gómez Juárez]].{{Cite web|date=2021-10-06|title=Hughes fuera, Silvana Gomez Juarez enfrenta a Lupita Godinez en UFC Vegas more|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Farah Hannoun|date=2021-07-07|access-date=2021-07-07}}\n\n==Background==\nAfter a one-year hiatus due to the [[COVID-19 pandemic]], the promotion announced the return of 'International Fight Week', which took place in [[Las Vegas]] during the days leading up to and surrounding the event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.ufc.com/news/9th-annual-ufc-international-fight-week-takes-over-las-vegas-september-21-25|title=9th annual UFC International Fight Week takes over Las Vegas, September 21–25|publisher=ufc.com|author=Staff|date=2021-07-20|access-date=2021-07-20}}\n\nA [[UFC Light Heavyweight Championship]] bout between current champion [[Jan Błachowicz]] (also former [[Konfrontacja Sztuk Walki#Light heavyweight championship|KSW Light Heavyweight Champion]]) and former title challenger [[Glover Teixeira]] was scheduled take place at the event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/31255723/jan-blachowicz-defend-ufc-light-heavyweight-title-vs-glover-teixeira-ufc-266|title=Jan Blachowicz to defend UFC light heavyweight title vs. Glover Teixeira at UFC 266|work=espn.com|author=Brett Okamoto|date=2021-04-14|access-date=2021-04-14}} However, the bout was postponed in late June and moved to [[UFC 267]].{{Cite web|url=https://www.bjpenn.com/mma-news/jan-blachowicz-vs-glover-teixeira-delayed-now-headlines-ufc-267-in-abu-dhabi/|title=Jan Blachowicz vs. Glover Teixeira delayed, now headlines UFC 267 in Abu Dhabi|publisher=bjpenn.com|author=Adam Martin|date=2021-06-27|access-date=2021-06-28}}\n\nA [[UFC Featherweight Championship]] bout between current champion [[Alexander Volkanovski]] and former title challenger [[Brian Ortega]] headlined the event. The pairing was previously scheduled to take place at [[UFC 260]], but it was cancelled a week before taking place as Volkanovski [[COVID-19 testing|tested positive for COVID-19]].{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2021/3/20/22342440/alexander-volkanovski-out-of-ufc-260-due-covid-19-protocols-fight-against-brian-ortega-rescheduled|title=Alexander Volkanovski out of UFC 260 due to COVID-19 protocols, fight against Brian Ortega to be rescheduled|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2021-03-20|access-date=2021-06-09}} The pairing was then postponed a few months as both fighters were named head coaches for [[The Return of The Ultimate Fighter: Team Volkanovski vs. Team Ortega]].{{Cite web|url=https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/31185553/alexander-volkanovski-brian-ortega-named-coaches-ultimate-fighter|title=Volkanovski, Ortega named coaches for next TUF|work=espn.com|author=Brett Okamoto|date=2021-04-02|access-date=2021-04-02}}\n\nA [[UFC Women's Flyweight Championship]] bout between current champion [[Valentina Shevchenko]] and former [[Invicta Fighting Championships#Bantamweight Championship|Invicta FC Bantamweight Champion]] [[Lauren Murphy]] served as the co-headliner.{{Cite web|url=https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/31814276/alexander-volkanovski-valentina-shevchenko-set-defend-titles-ufc-266|title=Alexander Volkanovski, Valentina Shevchenko set to defend titles at UFC 266|work=espn.com|author=Brett Okamoto|date=2021-06-13|access-date=2021-06-13}}\n\nA middleweight bout between former [[UFC Welterweight Championship|UFC Welterweight Champion]] [[Robbie Lawler]] and former [[List of WEC champions#Welterweight Championship|WEC]] and [[List of Strikeforce champions#Welterweight Championship|Strikeforce Welterweight Champion]] [[Nick Diaz]] (also former welterweight title challenger) took place at this event, marking the second time a non-main event and non-title bout had been scheduled for five rounds.{{Cite web|url=https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/31813676/nick-diaz-joins-brother-nate-back-ufc-agrees-september-bout-vs-robbie-lawler-las-vegas|title=Nick Diaz joins brother Nate back in UFC, agrees to September bout vs. Robbie Lawler in Las Vegas|work=espn.com|author=Brett Okamoto|date=2021-07-13|access-date=2021-07-13}} They previously met 17 years ago at [[UFC 47]], with Diaz winning by knockout.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/07/ufc-266-nick-diaz-return-confirmed-robbie-lawler-rematch-five-rounds-non-title|title=Nick Diaz returns vs. Robbie Lawler in five-round UFC 266 co-headliner|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Mike Bohn|date=2021-07-13|access-date=2021-07-13}} The bout was originally expected to take place at welterweight, but was moved up a weight class days before the event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.bjpenn.com/mma-news/ufc/nick-diaz-vs-robbie-lawler-2-at-ufc-266-now-a-middleweight-fight/|title= Nick Diaz vs. Robbie Lawler 2 at UFC 266 now a middleweight fight|publisher=bjpenn.com|author=Cole Shelton|date=2021-09-21|access-date=2021-09-21}}\n\nA heavyweight bout between [[Shamil Abdurakhimov]] and [[Chris Daukaus]] took place at the event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2021/7/26/22595377/shamil-abdurakhimov-vs-chris-daukaus-scratched-from-ufc-vegas-33|title=Shamil Abdurakhimov vs. Chris Daukaus scratched from UFC Vegas 33, moved to UFC 266 instead|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Steven Marrocco|date=2021-07-26|access-date=2021-07-26}} The pairing was initially scheduled to meet on July 24, 2021, at [[UFC on ESPN: Sandhagen vs. Dillashaw]], but the matchup was removed from that card on July 19 due to [[COVID-19]] protocols within Abdurakhimov's camp.{{Cite web|title=Chris Daukaus vs. Shamil Abdurakhimov pulled from UFC Vegas 32, fight now expected to take place on July 31|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2021/7/19/22583759/chris-daukaus-vs-shamil-abdurakhimov-pulled-from-ufc-vegas-32-fight-expected-take-place-on-july-31|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2021-07-19|access-date=2021-07-19}} The matchup remained intact and was rescheduled for the following week at [[UFC on ESPN: Hall vs. Strickland]], but it was postponed again due to undisclosed reasons.{{Cite web|title=Chris Daukaus vs. Shamil Abdurakhimov scratched from UFC Vegas 33|url=https://mymmanews.com/chris-daukaus-vs-shamil-abdurakhimov-scratched-from-ufc-vegas-33/|publisher=mymmanews.com|author=Adam Martin|date=2021-07-26|access-date=2021-07-26}}\n\nA women's flyweight bout between [[The Ultimate Fighter: Team Joanna vs. Team Cláudia]] strawweight winner [[Tatiana Suarez]] and former title challenger [[Roxanne Modafferi]] was scheduled for the event.{{Cite challengers [[Paulo Costa]] and [[Marvin Vettori]] headlined the event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2021/8/2/22606986/paulo-costa-vs-marvin-vettori-headlines-oct-23-ufc-event|title=Paulo As a result, he forfeited 20% of his fight purse to Vettori.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/10/ufc-fight-night-196-news-paulo-costa-fined-catchweight-vs-marvin-vettori|title=Paulo headlined the event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2021/9/29/22701023/ketlen-vieira-miesha-tate-rebooked-main-event-ufc-vegas-43|title=Ketlen Vieira vs. Miesha Tate rebooked as UFC Vegas was pulled from the card when Tate [[COVID-19 testing|tested positive for COVID-19]].{{Cite and the pairing was postponed.{{Cite web|url=https://www.superlutas.com.br/noticias/156432/brasileiro-rani-yahya-testa-positivo-para-covid-19-e-deixa-luta-no-ufc-vegas-33/|title=After rejecting 2]]\n|followingevent= [[UFC Fight Night: Vieira vs. Tate]]\n}}\n\n'''''UFC Fight Night: Holloway vs. Rodríguez''''' (also known as '''''UFC Fight Night 197''''', '''''UFC Vegas 42''''' and '''''UFC on ESPN+ 55''''') was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that took place on November 13, 2021, at the [[UFC Apex]] facility in [[Enterprise, Nevada]], part of the [[Las Vegas–Paradise, NV MSA|Las Vegas Metropolitan Area]], United States.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/lists/ufc-events-approved-by-nevada-state-athletic-commission-october-november-las-vegas|title=NAC approves six UFC events stretching from October through November|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2021-09-23|access-date=2021-09-24}}\n\n==Background==\nOn October 19, it was announced that the UFC sent a memo to fighters and their staff regarding policy changes to U.S. international travel requirements. Starting on November 8, foreign individuals looking to travel to the United States will need to be fully vaccinated with, and provide proof of, one of seven [[World Health Organization]] approved vaccines. They will also need a negative COVID-19 test from a 72-hour period prior to travel. The policy also applies to those crossing from Canada or Mexico, or those coming by ship. This was the first event under such requirement.{{cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2021/10/19/22735133/ufc-alerts-international-fighters-about-vaccination-requirements-to-travel-into-us-starting-nov-8|title=UFC alerts international fighters about new vaccination requirements to travel into the U.S. starting Nov. 8|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2021-10-19|access-date=2021-10-19}}{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/10/ufc-news-covid-19-new-policy-travel-hunter-campbell|title=UFC informs fighters of U.S. government's new COVID-19 vaccination travel policies|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2021-10-19|access-date=2021-10-19}}\n\nA featherweight bout between former [[UFC Featherweight Champion]] [[Max Holloway]] and [[The Ultimate Fighter: Latin America]] featherweight winner [[Yair Rodríguez]] served as the event headliner.{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/08/ufc-news-dana-white-max-holloway-vs-yair-rodriguez-november-13|title=UFC targeting Max Holloway vs. Yair Rodriguez as Nov. 13 main event|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2021-08-03|access-date=2021-08-03}} The pairing was previously scheduled to headline [[UFC on ESPN: Makhachev vs. Moisés]], but Holloway was pulled from the bout during the weeks leading up to that event due to injury.{{Cite web|url=https://espndeportes.espn.com/mma/nota?id=8790695|title=Max Holloway is injured and his fight against Yair Rodríguez is postponed|publisher=espndeportes.espn.com|author=Carlos Contreras Legaspi|date=2021-06-17|access-date=2021-06-17}} {{in lang|es}}\n\nA light heavyweight bout between [[Kennedy Nzechukwu]] and [[Da Un Jung]] was originally expected to take place at [[UFC Fight Night: Ladd vs. Dumont]], but it was pushed back to this event for unknown reasons.{{Cite web|url=https://www.rotowire.com/mma/player.php?id=1699 |title=Fight pushed back |publisher=rotowire.com|author=Staff|date=2021-10-07|access-date=2021-10-07}}\n\nA women's flyweight bout between former [[UFC Women's Flyweight Championship]] challenger [[Jessica Eye]] and [[Andrea Lee (fighter)|Andrea Lee]] was scheduled for the event.{{Cite by [[Cynthia Calvillo]].{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/10/jessica-eye-out-injury-illness-cynthia-calvillo-vs-andrea-lee-ufc-fight-night-197-women-mma-matchup|title=With Jessica Eye middleweight bouts between [[Kevin Holland]] and [[Kyle Daukaus]], as well as [[Eryk Anders]] and [[Roman Dolidze]] were scheduled for the event. However, Holland withdrew from his bout due to injury,{{Cite web|author=Damon Martin|date=2021-10-27|title=Kevin Holland injured, out of upcoming Kyle Daukaus rematch at UFC event on Nov. 13|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2021/10/27/22749633/kevin-holland-injured-out-of-upcoming-kyle-daukaus-rematch-at-ufc-event-on-nov-13|access-date=2021-10-28|publisher=mmafighting.com}} and Anders withdrew from the event for undisclosed reasons. Daukaus and Dolidze were then scheduled to face each other instead.{{Cite web|date=2021-10-29|title=With Eryk Anders out, Roman Dolidze vs. Kyle Daukaus targeted for UFC Fight Night 197|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/10/ufc-news-eryk-anders-out-roman-dolidze-vs-kyle-daukaus|access-date=2021-10-30|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King and John Morgan}} In turn, the bout was scrapped due to [[COVID-19]] protocols related to Dolidze's camp.{{Cite web|title= Kyle Daukaus vs. Roman Dolidze scrapped from UFC Vegas 42 card|url= https://www.mmafighting.com/2021/11/10/22773771/kyle-daukaus-vs-roman-dolidze-scrapped-from-ufc-vegas-42-card |date=2021-11-10|access-date=2021-11-10|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Damon Martin}}\n\nA lightweight bout between [[Marc Diakiese]] and [[Rafael Alves (fighter)|Rafael Alves]] was rescheduled for the event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmadna.nl/marc-diakiese-vs-rafael-alves-opnieuw-ingepland-ditmaal-voor-13-november-in-las-vegas/|title=Marc Diakiese vs. Rafael Alves rescheduled, this time for November 13 in Las pulled out of the fight in mid-August due to undisclosed reasons and the bout was scrapped.{{Cite web|url=https://mma.uno/marc-diakiese-vs-rafael-alves-reprogramado-para-ufc-vegas-42/|title=Marc Diakiese vs. Rafael Alves rescheduled for UFC Vegas 42|publisher=mma.uno|author=Steve Duncan|date=2021-10-28|access-date=2021-10-29}} {{in lang|es}}\n\nA featherweight bout between [[Lando Vannata]] and Tucker Lutz was expected to take place at this event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmadna.nl/lando-vannata-treft-tucker-lutz-tijdens-ufc-evenement-op-13-november/|title=Lando Vannata meets Tucker Lutz at respective bouts. [[Joel Álvarez]] weighed in at 157.5 pounds, one and a half pounds over the lightweight non-title fight limit. [[Liana Jojua]] weighed in at 128.5 pounds, two and a half pounds over the women's flyweight non-title fight limit. Both bouts proceeded at catchweight with Álvarez and Jojua each fined 30% of their purses, which went to their opponents [[Thiago Moisés]] and [[Cortney Casey]] respectively.{{Cite Rodriguez]]\n|followingevent= [[UFC Fight Night: Costa vs. Vettori]]\n}}\n\n'''''UFC Fight Night: Ladd vs. Dumont''''' (also known as '''''UFC Fight Night 195''''', '''''UFC Vegas 40''''' and '''''UFC on ESPN+ 53''''') was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that took place on October 16, 2021, at the [[UFC Apex]] facility in [[Enterprise, Nevada|Enterprise]], Nevada, part of the [[Las Vegas–Paradise, NV MSA|Las Vegas Metropolitan Area]], United States.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/lists/ufc-events-approved-by-nevada-state-athletic-commission-october-november-las-vegas|title=NAC approves six UFC events stretching from October through November|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2021-09-23|access-date=2021-09-24}}\n\n==Background==\nA women's bantamweight bout between [[Ketlen Vieira]] and former [[Strikeforce Women's Bantamweight Championship|Strikeforce]] and [[UFC Women's Bantamweight Championship|UFC Women's Bantamweight Champion]] [[Miesha Tate]] was expected to headline the event.{{Cite web|url=https://cagesidepress.com/2021/08/21/miesha-tate-vs-ketlen-vieira-set-for-october-16-ufc-event/|title=Miesha Tate vs. Ketlen Vieira set for October 16 UFC event|publisher=cagesidepress.com|author=Alex Behunin|date=2021-08-21|access-date=2021-08-22}} However, on September 22, the bout was pulled from the card after Tate [[COVID-19 testing|tested positive for COVID-19]].{{Cite web|url= https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/09/ufc-news-miesha-tate-positive-covid-19-ketlen-vieira-main-event-postponed|title= Miesha Tate tests positive for COVID-19; UFC headliner vs. Ketlen Vieira postponed|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Mike Bohn|date=2021-09-22|access-date=2021-09-22}} In turn, a women's featherweight bout between fellow former bantamweight champion [[Holly Holm]] and [[Norma Dumont]] was elevated to the main event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.abqjournal.com/2432315/mma-holms-oct-16-fight-to-be-the-main-event.html|title=MMA: Holm's Oct. 16 fight to be the main event|publisher=abqjournal.com|author=Rick Wright|date=2021-09-24|access-date=2021-09-24}} However on October 6, it was reported that Holm was forced to pull out of the event due to injury.{{Cite web|url=https://arielhelwani.substack.com/p/holly-holm-out-of-next-weekends-main|title=Holly Holm out of next weekend's main event (and other news)|publisher=arielhelwani.substack.com|author=Ariel Helwani|date=2021-10-06|access-date=2021-10-06}} She was replaced by [[Aspen Ladd]].{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/10/ufc-news-aspen-ladd-replaces-holly-holm-main-event-norma-dumont-oct-16-featherweight|title=Aspen Ladd replaces Holly Holm, meets Norma Dumont in new UFC Fight Night 195 main event|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Mike Bohn and John Morgan|date=2021-10-07|access-date=2021-10-07}}\n\nA bantamweight bout between [[Timur Valiev]] and Daniel Santos was expected to take place at the event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmadna.nl/timur-valiev-treft-daniel-willycat-santos-tijdens-ufc-evenement-op-16-oktober/|title=Timur Valiev faces Daniel \"Willycat\" Santos at UFC event on October 16|publisher=mmadna.nl|author=Marcel Dorff|date=2021-08-26|access-date=2021-09-21}} {{in lang|nl}} However, in mid-September, Valiev withdrew due to injury and was replaced by Marcelo Rojo.{{Cite web|url=https://mma.uno/timur-lesionado-marcelo-rojo-enfrenta-a-diego-santos-en-ufc-fight-night-del-16-de-octubre/|title=Timur injured, Marcelo Rojo faces Diego Santos at UFC Fight Night on October 16|publisher=mma.uno|author=Steve Duncan|date=2021-08-26|access-date=2021-09-21}} {{in lang|es}} In turn, Santos withdrew for unknown reasons and the bout was scrapped.{{Cite web|url=https://middleeasy.com/mma-rumors/aspen-ladd-rumor-mill/|title=Aspen Ladd cheating, crazy catchweights and more on the Sunday morning rumor mill|publisher=middleeasy.com|author=Chris De Santiago|date=2021-10-03|access-date=2021-10-09}}\n\nA women's flyweight bout between [[Manon Fiorot]] and [[Mayra Bueno Silva]] took place at this event.{{Cite web|url=https://mymmanews.com/ufc-266-loses-fight-as-manon-fiorot-vs-mayra-bueno-silva-scratched-due-to-covid-19-protocols/|title=UFC 266 loses fight as Manon Fiorot vs. Mayra Bueno Silva scratched due to COVID-19 protocols|publisher=mymmanews.com|author=Adam Martin|date=2021-09-22|access-date=2021-09-22}} The bout was originally scheduled for [[UFC 266]] but was postponed due to [[COVID-19]] protocols.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2021/6/9/22525971/manon-fiorot-to-fight-mayra-bueno-silva-at-sept-25-ufc-show|title=Manon Fiorot to fight Mayra Bueno Silva at Sept. 25 UFC show|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Guilherme Cruz and Alexander K. Lee|date=2021-06-09|access-date=2021-09-22}}\n\nA women's flyweight bout between [[Luana Carolina]] and [[Maryna Moroz]] was expected to take place at this event.{{Cite web|url=https://ge.globo.com/combate/noticia/ultimate-acerta-luana-dread-x-maryna-moroz-para-card-de-16-de-outubro.ghtml|title=Ultimate books Luana Dread vs. Maryna Moroz for October 16 card|publisher=ge.globo.com|author=Raphael Marinho|date=2021-07-06|access-date=2021-07-06}} {{in lang|pt}} However in late September, Moroz pulled out of the bout and was replaced by [[Sijara Eubanks]].{{Cite web|date=2021-09-28|title=Sijara Eubanks replaces Maryna Moroz vs. Luana Carolina at UFC Vegas 40|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2021/9/28/22698943/sijara-eubanks-replaces-maryna-moroz-luana-carolina-ufc-vegas-40|access-date=2021-09-29|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Guilherme Cruz}} In turn, Eubanks was pulled from the event due to COVID-19 protocols and replaced by [[Lupita Godinez]]. Godinez made history as the first fighter to compete twice in the span of seven days, a record in modern UFC, non-tournament fights. The previous fastest turnaround was 10 days, when [[Khamzat Chimaev]] competed against [[John Phillips (fighter)|John Phillips]] and Rhys McKee in July 2020.{{Cite web|date=2021-10-13|title=Loopy Godinez gets record turnaround, replaces Sijara Eubanks at UFC Fight Night 195|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/10/ufc-news-lupita-godinez-gets-quick-turnaround-replaces-sijara-eubanks-at-ufc-fight-night-195|access-date=2021-10-13|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Danny Segura}}\n\nA light heavyweight bout between [[Kennedy Nzechukwu]] and [[Da Un Jung]] was expected to take place at the event.{{Cite web|url=https://cagesidepress.com/2021/09/01/kennedy-nzechukwu-vs-da-un-jung-set-for-october-16-ufc-event/|title=Kennedy Nzechukwu vs. Da Un Jung Set For October 16 UFC Event |publisher=cagesidepress.com|author=Alex Behunin|date=2021-09-01|access-date=2021-10-07}} However, the bout was postponed to [[UFC Fight Night: Holloway vs. Rodríguez]] for unknown reasons.{{Cite web|url=https://www.rotowire.com/mma/player.php?id=1699|title=Fight pushed back |publisher=rotowire.com|author=Staff|date=2021-10-07|access-date=2021-10-07}}\n\nAt the weigh-ins, [[Julian Marquez]] was pulled from his middleweight bout against [[Jordan Wright]] due to \"non-COVID health issues.\" He never made it to the scale, even though his opponent weighed in within the middleweight non-title fight limit. As a result, the bout was cancelled.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/10/ufc-fight-night-195-aspen-ladd-norma-dumont-in-results-live-video-stream|title=UFC Fight Night 195 weigh-in ESPN: Barboza vs. Chikadze]]\n}}\n\n'''''UFC on ESPN: Cannonier vs. Gastelum''''' (also known as '''''UFC on ESPN 29''''' and '''''UFC Vegas 34''''') was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that took place on August 21, 2021, at the [[UFC Apex]] facility in [[Enterprise, Nevada]], part of the [[Las Vegas–Paradise, NV MSA|Las Vegas Metropolitan Area]], United States.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/lists/ufc-news-nac-approves-august-events-dana-white-contender-series|title=NAC approves two UFC events for August|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2021-07-07|access-date=2021-07-07}}{{Cite web|url=https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/31435529/paulo-costa-jared-cannonier-bout-being-finalized-headline-ufc-fight-night-august|title=Paulo Costa-Jared Cannonier bout being finalized to headline UFC Fight Night in August|publisher=espn.com|author=Brett Okamoto|date=2021-05-12|access-date=2021-05-12}}\n\n==Background==\nA middleweight bout between former [[UFC Middleweight Championship]] challenger [[Paulo Costa (fighter)|Paulo Costa]] and [[Jared Cannonier]] was expected headline this event. However, on June 4, Costa withdrew from the bout for unknown reasons and was replaced by former interim title challenger and [[The Ultimate Fighter: Team Jones vs. Team Sonnen]] middleweight winner [[Kelvin Gastelum]].{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2021/6/5/22519932/paulo-costa-out-kelvin-gastelum-steps-in-to-fight-jared-cannonier-in-aug-21-ufc-main-event|title=Paulo Costa out, Kelvin Gastelum steps in to fight Jared Cannonier in Aug. 21 UFC main event|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Alexander K. Lee and Damon Martin|date=2021-06-05|access-date=2021-06-05}} Costa later claimed he never signed the bout agreement and had issues with his payment, while not confirming them as the reason for his withdrawal.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2021/6/5/22520011/paulo-costa-never-signed-contract-jared-cannonier-fight-youtubers-are-exposing-issues-fighter-pay|title=Paulo Costa says he never signed contract for Jared Cannonier fight, claims ‘YouTubers’ are exposing issues with fighter pay|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Alexander K. Lee|date=2021-06-05|access-date=2021-06-05}}\n\nA heavyweight bout between [[Chase Sherman]] and [[Parker Porter]] took place at this event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2021/5/8/22426856/chase-sherman-vs-parker-porter-rebooked-for-ufc-event-on-aug-21|title=Chase Sherman vs. Parker Porter rebooked for UFC event on Aug. 21|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Mike Heck|date=2021-05-08|access-date=2021-05-10}} The pairing was previously scheduled to take place at [[UFC on ESPN: Whittaker vs. Gastelum]], but Porter was removed from that contest due to undisclosed reasons.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2021/4/7/22372820/with-parker-porter-out-andrei-arlovski-steps-in-to-face-chase-sherman-at-ufc-vegas-24|title=With Parker Porter out, Andrei Arlovski steps in to face Chase Sherman at UFC Vegas 24|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Mike Heck and Damon Martin|date=2021-04-07|access-date=2021-05-10}}\n\n[[Brian Kelleher]] and [[Domingo Pilarte (fighter)|Domingo Pilarte]] met in a bantamweight bout at this event.{{Cite web|url=https://fighterpath.com/2021/05/17/breaking-brian-kelleher-vs-domingo-pilarte-set-for-ufc-event-on-august-21/|title=Brian Kelleher vs Domingo Pilarte set for UFC event on August 21|publisher=fighterpath.com|author=Sergio Pinheiro|date=2021-05-17|access-date=2021-05-18}} The pairing was previously scheduled to take place in November 2018 at [[UFC 230]], but Pilarte pulled out due to injury.{{Cite news|url=http://mmadna.nl/montel-jackson-vervangt-domingo-pilarte-tijdens-ufc-230-tegen-brian-kelleher/|title=Montel Jackson replaces Domingo Pilarte during UFC 230 against Brian Kelleher|publisher=mmadna.nl|author=Marcel Dorff|date=2018-10-10|access-date=2021-05-18}} {{in lang|nl}}\n\nA bantamweight bout between Mana Martinez and Jesse Strader was expected to take place at this event.{{Cite web|url= https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/05/ufc-news-mana-martinez-vs-jesse-strader-august-21|title=Mana Martinez set for UFC debut, meets Jesse Strader on Aug. 21|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King and Farrah Hannoun|date=2021-05-31|access-date=2021-08-12}} However, just over a week before the event, Strader withdrew from the bout for unknown reasons and was replaced by [[Trevin Jones]].{{Cite web|url=https://cagesidepress.com/2021/08/12/jesse-strader-trevin-jones-mana-martinez-ufc-vegas-34/|title=Jesse Strader out, Trevin Jones in against Mana Martinez at UFC Vegas 34|publisher=cagesidepress.com|author=Jay Anderson|date=2021-08-12|access-date=2021-08-12}} In turn, Martinez pulled out during fight week due to undisclosed reasons.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/08/ufc-on-espn-29-news-mana-martinez-out-trevin-jones|title=Mana Martinez off UFC on ESPN 29; Trevin Jones needs new web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2021/8/17/22628848/with-mana-martinez-out-trevin-jones-welcomes-newcomer-said-yokub-at-ufc-vegas-34|title=With Mana Martinez out, Trevin Jones welcomes newcomer Said Yokub at UFC Vegas 34|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Mike Heck|date=2021-08-17|access-date=2021-08-17}} At the weigh-ins, Kakhramonov weighed in at 138.5 pounds, two and a half pounds over the bantamweight non-title fight limit. The bout proceeded at a catchweight and he forfeited 20% of his purse to Jones.{{Cite web|url= https://www.mmafighting.com/2021/8/20/22634285/ufc-vegas-34-weigh-in-results-kelvin-gastelum-needs-additional-hour-make-weight-hits-mark-main-event|title= UFC Vegas 34 weigh-in results: Kelvin Gastelum needs additional time to make weight, hits mark for main event|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Alexander K. Lee|date=2021-08-20|access-date=2021-08-20}}\n\nA women's flyweight bout between [[Cortney Casey]] and [[Liana Jojua]] in women's flyweight was scheduled for this event.{{Cite web|date=2021-07-09|title=UFC books Cortney Casey vs. Liana Jojua for Aug. 21 event|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/07/ufc-news-cortney-casey-vs-liana-jojua-august-21-women-mma-fight-booking-matchup|access-date=2021-07-09|author=Nolan King|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com}} However, Jojua was forced out of the fight due to visa issues. October 30, 2021, at the [[Etihad Arena]] in [[Abu Dhabi]], United Arab Emirates.{{Cite web|url=https://www.lowking.pl/doniesienia-jan-blachowicz-vs-glover-teixeira-na-ufc-267-w-abu-zabi/|title=Reports: Jan Błachowicz vs. Glover Teixeira at UFC 267 in Abu 30|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Farah Hannoun|date=2021-08-10|access-date=2021-08-10}}\n\n==Background==\nThis event took place in the evening in Abu Dhabi, which was the morning and afternoon in North America.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmamania.com/2021/8/3/22608570/ufc-267-abc-dana-white-blachowicz-teixieira-mma-news|title=UFC 267 will air on ABC if Dana White has his way|publisher=mmamania.com|author=Shakiel Mahjouri|date=2021-08-03|access-date=2021-10-14}} It was the first numbered UFC event that was not [[pay-per-view]] in the United States since [[UFC 138]] in 2011.{{Cite web|url=https://cagesidepress.com/2021/07/24/ufc-267-feature-double-header-non-ppv-united-states/|title=UFC 267 targeted as double-header, non-PPV event|publisher=cagesidepress.com|author=Gabriel Gonzalez|date=2021-07-24|access-date=2021-07-25}}\n\nA [[UFC Light Heavyweight Championship]] bout between current champion [[Jan Błachowicz]] (also former [[Konfrontacja Sztuk Walki#Light heavyweight championship|KSW Light Heavyweight Champion]]) and former title challenger [[Glover Teixeira]] was expected to take place at [[UFC 266]].{{Cite web|url=https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/31255723/jan-blachowicz-defend-ufc-light-heavyweight-title-vs-glover-teixeira-ufc-266|title=Jan Blachowicz to defend UFC light heavyweight title vs. Glover web|url=https://www.bjpenn.com/mma-news/jan-blachowicz-vs-glover-teixeira-delayed-now-headlines-ufc-267-in-abu-dhabi/|title=Jan Blachowicz vs. Glover Teixeira delayed, now headlines UFC 267 in Abu Dhabi|publisher=bjpenn.com|author=Adam Martin|date=2021-06-27|access-date=2021-06-28}} Former [[Rizin Fighting Federation#Light Heavyweight Championship|Rizin Light Heavyweight Champion]] [[Jiří Procházka]] served as backup and potential replacement for this fight.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/08/ufc-267-jiri-prochazka-backup-for-jan-blachowicz-vs-glover-teixeira|title=Jiri Prochazka to serve as UFC 267 backup for Jan Blachowicz vs. Glover Sterling]] and the former champion [[Petr Yan]] was expected to take place at this event.{{Cite fight (and title) by disqualification (intentional illegal knee strike) in the fourth round, becoming the first fighter to win a UFC title by disqualification.{{Cite web|url=https://www.cbssports.com/mma/news/ufc-259-results-highlights-aljamain-sterling-earns-135-pound-title-after-petr-yans-illegal-knee-leads-to-dq/|title=UFC 259 results: Aljamain Sterling earns just hours before taking place due to [[COVID-19]] protocol issues in Amedovski's camp.{{Cite web|url=https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/31795790/ufc-middleweight-fight-yu-haozong-alen-amedovski-due-covid-19-protocols|title=Yaozong-Amedovski UFC bout off due to protocols|work=espn.com|author=Marc Raimondi|date=2021-07-10|access-date=2021-07-10}} However, Amedovski was removed from the event due to undisclosed reasons and replaced by [[Andre Petroski]].{{Cite place at the event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmasucka.com/2021/08/20/tristan-connelly-makwan-amirkhani-ufc-267/|title= Tristan Connelly vs. Makwan Amirkhani face off at UFC [[Madison Square Garden]] in [[New York City]], [[New York (state)|New York]], United States.{{Cite web|url=https://www.bloodyelbow.com/2021/8/28/22646610/ufc-268-set-for-new-york-city-justin-gaethje-vs-michael-chandler-confirmed-mma-news|title=UFC 268 set for New York City; Gaethje vs. Chandler, Namajunas vs. Zhang 2 confirmed|publisher=bloodyelbow.com|author=Milan Ordoñez|date=2021-08-28|access-date=2021-08-29|archive-date=2021-08-29|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20210829110953/https://www.bloodyelbow.com/2021/8/28/22646610/ufc-268-set-for-new-york-city-justin-gaethje-vs-michael-chandler-confirmed-mma-news|url-status=dead}}\n\n==Background==\nA [[UFC Welterweight Championship]] rematch between the current champion [[Kamaru Usman]] (also [[The Ultimate Fighter: American Top Team vs. Blackzilians]] welterweight winner) and former interim champion [[Colby Covington]] headlined the event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmamania.com/2021/7/26/22594956/kamaru-usman-vs-colby-covington-2-ufc-268-november-mma-news|title=Usman vs. Covington 2 set for UFC 268|publisher=mmamania.com|author=Shakiel Mahjouri|date=2021-07-26|access-date=2021-07-27}} Serving as the event headliner, they previously met at [[UFC 245]] in December 2019, where Usman defended his title via fifth-round technical knockout.{{Cite web|url=https://www.cbssports.com/mma/news/ufc-245-results-highlights-kamaru-usman-outlasts-colby-covington-in-hellacious-war-for-late-tko-victory/|title=UFC 245 results, highlights: Kamaru Usman outlasts Colby Covington in hellacious war for late TKO victory|publisher=cbssports.com|author=Brian Campbell and Brent Brookhouse|date=2019-12-15|access-date=2021-08-29}} [[Vicente Luque]] served as backup and potential replacement for this fight.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2021/11/3/22762497/vicente-luque-backup-fighter-ufc-268-kamaru-usman-colby-covington|title=Vicente Luque to serve as backup fighter for UFC 268 headliner Kamaru Usman vs. Colby Covington|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Guilherme Cruz|date=2021-11-03|access-date=2021-11-03}} At the weigh-ins, Luque weighed in at 172.2 pounds, 2.2 pounds over the welterweight title fight limit making him ineligible to compete for the title.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2021/11/5/22765231/ufc-268-weigh-in-results-two-title-fights-set-main-event-backup-vicente-luque-two-others-miss-weight|title=UFC 268 weigh-in results: Two title fights set; Main event backup Vicente Luque, two others miss weight|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Alexander K. Lee|date=2021-11-05|access-date=2021-11-05}}\n\nA [[UFC Women's Strawweight Championship]] rematch between current two-time champion [[Rose Namajunas]] and former champion [[Zhang Weili]] took place as the co-main event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/08/ufc-268-rose-namajunas-vs-zhang-weili-2-covington-usman-new-york-city|title=Rose Namajunas vs. Zhang Weili 2 among latest additions to official UFC 268 card|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2021-08-28|access-date=2021-08-29}} They previously met at [[UFC 261]], where Namajunas won the title via first-round knockout.{{Cite web|url=https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/31328088/rose-namajunas-wins-ufc-strawweight-title-shocking-knockout-zhang-weili|title=Rose Namajunas wins UFC's strawweight title with shocking knockout of Zhang Weili|work=espn.com|author=Brett Okamoto|date=2021-04-25|access-date=2021-08-29}}\n\nA women’s bantamweight bout between former [[UFC Women's Featherweight Championship|UFC Women's Featherweight Champion]] [[Germaine de Randamie]] and [[Irene Aldana]] was expected to take place at the event.{{Cite web|url= https://cagesidepress.com/2021/08/28/germaine-de-randamie-vs-irene-aldana-added-ufc-268-nyc|title= Germaine de Randamie vs. Irene Aldana Added to UFC 268 in NYC|publisher=cagesidepress.com|author=Jay Anderson|date=2021-08-28|access-date=2021-09-08}} However, de Randamie withdrew in early September due to injury.{{Cite web|url= https://www.essentiallysports.com/ufc-mma-news-breaking-germaine-de-randamie-out-of-her-ufc-268-fight-against-irene-aldana/|title=BREAKING: Germaine de Randamie Out of Her UFC 268 Fight Against Irene Aldana |publisher=essentiallysports.com|author=Darryl Pinto|date=2021-09-08|access-date=2021-09-08}}\n\nA middleweight bout between former [[List of Strikeforce champions#Middleweight Championship|Strikeforce]] and [[UFC Middleweight Championship|UFC Middleweight Champion]] [[Luke Rockhold]] and [[Sean Strickland]] was expected to take place at the event.{{Cite web|url=https://empiresportsmedia.com/mma/ufc-books-luke-rockhold-sean-strickland/|title=UFC books Luke Rockhold – Sean Strickland|publisher=empiresportsmedia.com|author=Daniel Cunningham|date=2021-08-04|access-date=2021-08-04}} However on October 11, Rockhold withdrew due to a [[spinal disc hernia|herniated disc]].{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2021/10/8/22716518/phil-hawes-vs-chris-curtis-short-notice-bout-off-of-ufc-vegas-39|title=Phil Hawes vs. Chris Curtis short-notice bout off of UFC Vegas Laramie was scheduled for the event.{{Cite web|url=https://cagesidepress.com/2021/08/31/melsik-baghdasaryan-vs-tj-laramie-added-to-ufc-268/|title=Melsik Baghdasaryan vs. TJ Laramie added to UFC 268|publisher=cagesidepress.com|author= Alex Behunin|date=2021-08-31|access-date=2021-08-31}} However, Laramie was forced to pull out after he was diagnosed with an [[Methicillin-resistant Staphylococcus aureus|MRSA]] infection.{{Cite web|date=2021-10-23|title=T.J. Laramie announces UFC 268 withdrawal after MRSA diagnosis, shares graphic images|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/10/ufc-268-tj-laramie-mrsa-withdrawal-melsik-baghdasaryan|access-date=2021-10-23|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King}} He was replaced by promotional newcomer Bruno Souza.{{Cite web|url=https://mmadna.nl/breaking-lfa-kampioen-bruno-souza-treft-melsik-baghdasaryan-op-6-november-in-new-york/|title=BREAKING: LFA Champion Bruno Souza meets Melsik Baghdasaryan on November 6 in New York|publisher=mmadna.nl|author=Marcel Dorff|date=2021-10-30|access-date=2021-10-30}} {{in lang|nl}} At the weigh-ins, Souza weighed in at 148.4 pounds, 2.4 pounds over the featherweight non-title fight limit. The bout proceeded at a catchweight and he forfeited 20% of his purse to Baghdasaryan.\n\nA light heavyweight fight between [[Aleksa Camur]] and [[John Allan (fighter)|John Allan]] was scheduled for the event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.superlutas.com.br/noticias/157236/de-olho-na-primeira-vitoria-pelo-ultimate-john-allan-enfrenta-aleksa-camur-no-ufc-268/|title=With his sights on his first win at UFC, John Allan faces Aleksa Camur at UFC 268|publisher=superlutas.com.br|author=VH Gonzaga|date=2021-08-30|access-date=2021-11-01}} {{in lang|pt}} However, Camur pulled out of the bout citing an undisclosed injury and was replaced by [[Dustin Jacoby]].{{Cite web|url=https://mmadna.nl/breaking-dustin-jacoby-vervangt-aleksa-camur-tegen-john-allan-zaterdag-in-new-york/|title=Dustin Jacoby replaces Aleksa Camur against John Allan in New York on Saturday|publisher=mmadna.nl|author=Marcel Dorff|date=2021-11-01|access-date=2021-11-01}} {{in lang|nl}}\n\nAt the weigh-ins, [[C.J. Vergara]] weighed in at 127.4 pounds, 1.4 pounds over the flyweight non-title fight limit. His bout proceeded at a catchweight and he forfeited 20% of his purse to his opponent [[Ode' Osbourne]].\n\n==Results==\n{{MMAevent}}\n{{MMAevent card|Main card}}\n{{MMAevent Fight Night: Brunson vs. Till\n|image= Official_poster_for_UFC_Fight_Night_Brunson_vs._Till.jpg\n|promotion= [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]]\n|date= {{start date|2021|09|04}}\n|venue= [[UFC win, finishes list at middleweight|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Mike Bohn|date=2021-09-05|access-date=2021-09-05}}\n|gate=\n|previousevent= [[UFC on ESPN: Barboza vs. Chikadze]]\n|followingevent= [[UFC Fight Night: Smith vs. Spann]]\n}}\n\n'''''UFC Fight Night: Brunson vs. Till''''' (also known as '''''UFC Fight Night 191 ''''' and '''''UFC on ESPN+ 49''''' and '''''UFC Vegas 36''''') was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that took place on September 4, 2021, at the [[UFC Apex]] facility in [[Enterprise, Nevada]], part of the [[Las Vegas–Paradise, NV MSA|Las Vegas Metropolitan Area]], United States.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2021/8/2/22605692/ufc-moves-sept-4-event-london-las-vegas-ariane-lipski-replaces-taila-santos-mandy-bohm|title=UFC moves Sept. 4 event from London to Las Vegas, Ariane Lipski replaces Taila Santos vs. Mandy Bohm|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Guilherme Cruz|date=2021-08-02|access-date=2021-08-02}}\n\n==Background==\nWhile never officially announced by the organization, the promotion initially wanted to contest the event in [[London]]. It would have marked the promotion's first event in the [[United Kingdom]] since [[UFC Fight Night: Till vs. Masvidal]] in March 2019, as well as the first country other than the [[United Arab Emirates]] to host an international event after the pandemic effects began on mixed martial arts.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmamania.com/2021/6/4/22519423/report-darren-till-vs-derek-brunson-main-event-expected-to-be-moved-to-london-for-sept-4-ufc-mma|title=Darren Till vs. Derek Brunson main event expected to be moved to London for Sept. 4|publisher=mmamania.com|author=Dan Hiergesell|date=2021-06-04|access-date=2021-08-02}} However, negotiations with the British officials fizzled amongst [[COVID-19 pandemic in the United Kingdom|COVID-19 protocols]] and the promotion turned to their home base of Las Vegas.\n\nA middleweight bout between [[Derek Brunson]] and former [[UFC Welterweight Championship]] challenger [[Darren Till]] headlined the event.{{Cite web|author=Ryan Harkness|date=2021-08-07|title=Brunson vs. Till to headline Sept. 4 UFC Event|url=https://www.mmamania.com/2021/8/7/22614668/confirmed-derek-brunson-vs-darren-till-to-headline-september-4th-ufc-fight-night|access-date=2021-08-08|publisher=mmamania.com}} They were originally expected to serve as the main event for a planned event on August 14, but the date was scrapped.{{Cite web|url=https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/31435609/ufc-close-finalizing-darren-derek-brunson-bout-aug-14|title=UFC close to finalizing Darren Till-Derek Brunson bout for Aug. 14|work=espn.com|author=Brett Okamoto|date=2021-05-12|access-date=2021-05-12}}\n\nSeveral bouts were also booked to take place at the planned August 14 event, before being rescheduled to this date: \n*A women's flyweight bout between [[Taila Santos]] and Mandy Böhm.{{Cite web|url=https://globoesporte.globo.com/combate/noticia/ultimate-encaminha-luta-entre-taila-santos-e-mandy-bohm-para-agosto.ghtml|title=UFC sets fight between Taila Santos and Mandy Bohm for August|publisher=globoesporte.globo.com|author=Marcelo Barone|date=2021-05-24|access-date=2021-05-24}} {{in lang|pt}}{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2021/7/13/22575421/taila-santos-mandy-bohm-pushed-back-september-ufc-event-london|title=Taila Santos vs. Mandy Bohm pushed back to September UFC event London|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Guilherme Cruz|date=2021-07-13|access-date=2021-07-13}} In turn, after the change in location, Santos was removed due to alleged [[visa (document)|visa]] issues and replaced by former [[Konfrontacja Sztuk Walki#Women's flyweight championship|KSW Women's Flyweight Champion]] [[Ariane Lipski]]. Subsequently, the bout was removed from the card during the week leading up to the event as Böhm was sidelined due to illness. The pairing is expected to remain intact and be rescheduled two weeks later at [[UFC Fight Night: Smith vs. Spann]].{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2021/8/30/22648739/ariane-lipski-vs-mandy-bohm-off-of-ufc-vegas-36-rescheduled-for-sept-18|title=Ariane Lipski vs. Mandy Bohm off of UFC Vegas 36, rescheduled for Sept. 18|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Guilherme Cruz and Alexander K. Lee|date=2021-08-30|access-date=2021-08-30}}\n*A women's flyweight bout between [[Molly McCann]] and [[Ji Yeon Kim]].{{Cite web|url=https://mmadna.nl/molly-mccann-treft-ji-yeon-kim-op-14-augustus/|title=Molly McCann meets Ji Yeon Kim on August 14|publisher=mmadna.nl|author=Jamal Boussenaf|date=2021-05-17|access-date=2021-07-26}} {{in lang|nl}}{{Cite web|url=https://www.gnp1.de/ufc/news/wer-kaempft-gegen-wen-kw-27-2021|title=Who is fighting against whom? KW 27/2021|publisher=gnp1.de|author=Alexander Petzel-Gligorea|date=2021-07-11|access-date=2021-07-26}} {{in lang|de}}\n*A featherweight bout featuring [[Charles Jourdain]] and [[Lerone Murphy]].{{Cite web|url=https://mmadna.nl/frans-canadezen-krijgen-gevechten-tijdens-ufc-evenement-op-14-augustus/|title=French Canadians get fights at UFC event on August 14|publisher=mmadna.nl|author=Marcel Dorff\n|date=2021-05-24|access-date=2021-07-26}} {{in lang|nl}}{{Cite web|url=https://www.sherdog.com/news/news/Paddy-Pimblett-to-Make-UFC-Debut-vs-Luigi-Vendramini-on-Sept-4-180437|title=Paddy Pimblett to make UFC debut vs. Luigi Vendramini on Sept. 4|publisher=sherdog.com|author=Jay Pettry|date=2021-07-09|access-date=2021-07-26}} Murphy was eventually pulled from the event due to visa issues and replaced by [[Julian Erosa]], changing the contest to a catchweight bout of 150 pounds.{{Cite web|url=https://mmadna.nl/breaking-julian-erosa-nieuwe-tegenstander-van-charles-jourdain-op-4-september/|title=BREAKING: Julian Erosa new opponent of Charles Jourdain on September 4|publisher=mmadna.nl|author=Jamal Boussenaf|date=2021-08-25|access-date=2021-08-25}} {{in lang|nl}}\n*A middleweight bout between [[Marc-André Barriault]] and [[Dalcha Lungiambula]].{{Cite web|url=https://mymmanews.com/marc-andre-barriault-vs-dalcha-lungiambula-moved-to-ufc-london-as-august-14-card-officially-gets-canceled/|title=Marc-Andre Barriault vs. Dalcha Lungiambula moved to UFC London as August 14 card officially gets canceled|publisher=mymmanews.com|author=Cole Shelton|date=2021-07-25|access-date=2021-07-26}}\n\nA flyweight bout between former [[UFC Flyweight Championship]] challenger [[Alex Perez (fighter)|Alex Perez]] and [[Matt Schnell (fighter)|Matt Schnell]] had been rescheduled for this event.{{Cite web|url=https://mma.uno/alex-perez-vs-matt-schnell-reprogramado-para-ufc-fight-night-del-4-de-septiembre/|title=Alex Perez vs. Matt Schnell rescheduled for UFC Fight Night on September 4|publisher=mma.uno|author=Steve Duncan|date=2021-08-07|access-date=2021-08-07}} {{in lang|es}} They were originally expected to meet at [[UFC 262]], before Perez was forced to pull out due to undisclosed reasons.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/04/ufc-262-alex-perez-out-rogerio-bontorin-to-fight-matt-schnell|title=With Alex Perez out, Rogerio Bontorin targeted to fight Matt Schnell at UFC 262|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Farah Hannoun|date=2021-04-21|access-date=2021-04-22}} They were then booked again at [[UFC on ESPN: Barboza vs. Chikadze]], before being shifted to this event. However, the bout was yet again postponed for unknown reasons to [[UFC 269]].{{Cite web|last=King|first=Kristen|date=2021-09-20|title=Fights on Tap: Brandon Moreno vs. Deiveson Figueiredo 3 among 15 UFC bouts announced|url=https://www.bloodyelbow.com/2021/9/20/22682209/fights-on-tap-brandon-moreno-vs-deiveson-figueiredo-3-among-15-ufc-fights-announced-mma-news|access-date=2021-09-29|website=Bloody Elbow|language=en|archive-date=2022-05-10|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20220510215952/https://www.bloodyelbow.com/2021/9/20/22682209/fights-on-tap-brandon-moreno-vs-deiveson-figueiredo-3-among-15-ufc-fights-announced-mma-news|url-status=dead}}\n\n[[Nathaniel Wood (fighter)|Nathaniel Wood]] was expected to face [[Jonathan Martinez]] in a bantamweight bout at the event.{{Cite web|date=2021-07-01|author=Alfredo Zullino|title=Nathaniel Wood returns to action on September 4, faces Jonathan Martinez|url=https://www.bjpenn.com/mma-news/nathaniel-wood-returns-to-action-in-london-on-september-4-faces-jonathan-martinez/|access-date=2021-07-01|publisher=bjpenn.com}} However, Wood pulled out of the bout in mid-August citing a broken hand and was replaced by Marcelo Rojo.{{Cite web|url=https://mmadna.nl/jonathan-martinez-en-marcelo-rojo-treffen-elkaar-op-4-september/|title=Jonathan Martinez and Marcelo Rojo meet on September 4th|publisher=mmadna.nl|author=Marcel Dorff|date=2021-08-11|access-date=2021-08-11}} {{in lang|nl}} At the weigh-ins, Martinez weighed in at 138 pounds, two pounds over the bantamweight non-title fight limit. The bout was expected to proceed at a catchweight, but it was eventually scrapped hours later.{{Cite web|url=https://m.sherdog.com/news/news/UFC-Fight-Night-191-Weighin-Results-Brunson-vs-Till-Cleared-Jonathan-Martinez-Heavy-181509|title=UFC Fight Night 191 weigh-in results: Brunson vs. Till cleared; Jonathan Martinez heavy|publisher=sherdog.com|author=Tyler Treese|date=2021-09-03|access-date=2021-09-03}}{{Cite web|url= https://www.mmafighting.com/2021/9/3/22656644/jonathan-martinez-vs-marcelo-rojo-scratched-from-ufc-vegas-36-fight-card|title= Jonathan Martinez vs. Marcelo Rojo scratched from UFC Vegas 36 fight card|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Mike Heck|date=2021-09-03|access-date=2021-09-03}}\n\nA bantamweight bout between [[Jack Shore]] and [[Said Nurmagomedov]] was scheduled for the event.{{cite web|author=Alex Behunin|title=UFC: Jack Shore vs. Said Nurmagomedov set for September 4 in web|date=2021-08-26|title=With Zviad Lazishvili out, Liudvik Sholinian steps in to face Jack Shore at UFC Fight Night Night: Brunson vs. Till]]\n}}\n\n'''''UFC on ESPN: Barboza vs. Chikadze''''' (also known as '''''UFC on ESPN 30''''' and '''''UFC Vegas 35''''') was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that took place on August 28, 2021, at the [[UFC Apex]] facility in [[Enterprise, Nevada]], part of the [[Las Vegas–Paradise, NV MSA|Las Vegas Metropolitan Area]], United States.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/lists/ufc-news-nac-approves-august-events-dana-white-contender-series|title=NAC approves two UFC events for August|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2021-07-07|access-date=2021-07-07}}\n\n==Background==\nA featherweight bout between [[Edson Barboza]] and [[Giga Chikadze]] headlined the event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/07/ufc-news-edson-barboza-vs-giga-chikadze-august-28|title=UFC finalizing Edson Barboza vs. Giga Chikadze headliner for Aug. 28 event|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King and Mike Bohn|date=2021-07-05|access-date=2021-07-06}}\n\n[[The Return of The Ultimate Fighter: Team Volkanovski vs. Team Ortega]] season finale took place at this event, featuring the tournament finals of both the middleweight and bantamweight divisions.{{Cite web|url=https://mmadna.nl/tuf-bantamweight-finale-bekend-zal-plaatsvinden-tijdens-ufc-on-espn-30-in-las-vegas/|title=TUF Bantamweight Final announced, to be held at UFC on ESPN 30 in Las injury and was replaced by Gilbert Urbina.{{Cite Oliveira]] and former interim champion [[Dustin Poirier]] served as the event headliner.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/10/ufc-269-dustin-poirier-vs-charles-oliveira-booked-dec-11|title=Dustin Poirier to challenge Charles Oliveira for lightweight title at UFC current [[UFC Women's Featherweight Championship|UFC Women's Featherweight Champion]]) and [[The Ultimate Fighter: Team Rousey vs. Team Tate]] bantamweight winner [[Julianna Peña]] took place at the event.{{Cite web|date=2021-08-30|title=Amanda Nunes vs. Julianna Pena rescheduled for UFC 269|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2021/8/30/22648989/amanda-nunes-vs-julianna-pena-rescheduled-for-ufc-269|access-date=2021-08-30|author=Alexander K. Lee|publisher=mmafighting.com}} The pairing was previously scheduled to take place at [[UFC 265]], but it was postponed after Nunes [[COVID-19 testing|tested positive for COVID-19]].{{Cite web|url= https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/31912707/bantamweight-champion-amanda-nunes-tests-positive-covid-19-august-title-bout-off|title= Amanda Nunes tests positive for COVID-19; Bantamweight title and former champion [[Deiveson Figueiredo]] was expected to take place at the event.{{Cite web|url= https://www.mmafighting.com/2021/9/15/22676833/brandon-moreno-vs-deiveson-figueiredo-trilogy-booked-for-ufc-269-in-december|title=Brandon Moreno vs. Deiveson Figueiredo trilogy booked for UFC 269 in December|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2021-09-15|access-date=2021-09-15}} The pairing first met at [[UFC 256]] on December 12, 2020, resulting in a majority draw (Figueiredo retained the title).{{Cite web|url=https://www.cbssports.com/mma/news/ufc-256-results-highlights-deiveson-figueiredo-brandon-moreno-fight-to-a-draw-in-an-instant-classic/live/|title=UFC 256 results, highlights: Deiveson Figueiredo, Brandon Moreno fight to a draw in an instant classic|publisher=cbssports.com|author=Brent Brookhouse|date=2021-12-13|access-date=2021-09-15}} Their second meeting took place at [[UFC 263]] on June 12, where Moreno captured the title by submission in the third round.{{Cite web|url= https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/06/ufc-263-results-brandon-moreno-taps-deiveson-figueiredo-becomes-first-mexico-born-champion|title=UFC 263 results: Brandon Moreno taps Deiveson Figueiredo, becomes first Mexico-born champion|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Matthew Wells|date=2021-07-13|access-date=2021-10-11}} Subsequently, the bout was moved to [[UFC 270]] due to the addition of a welterweight bout between [[Jorge Masvidal]] and [[Leon Edwards]].{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/10/ufc-news-jorge-masvidal-vs-leon-edwards-in-the-works-for-ufc-269|title=Jorge Masvidal vs. Leon Edwards set to meet at final pay-per-view of the year at UFC 269|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Matthew Wells and Mike Bohn|date=2021-10-11|access-date=2021-10-11}} Masvidal later withdrew due to injury in early November and the bout was scrapped.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/11/ufc-269-news-jorge-masvidal-vs-leon-edwards-off-injury|title=Jorge Masvidal out of UFC 269 fight vs. Leon Edwards|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King and Staff|date=2021-11-10|access-date=2021-11-10}}\n\nA flyweight bout between former title challenger [[Alex Perez (fighter)|Alex Perez]] and [[Matt Schnell]] had been rescheduled for [[UFC Fight Night: Brunson vs. Till]].{{Cite web|author=Steve Duncan|date=2021-08-07|title=Alex Perez vs. Matt Schnell rescheduled for UFC Fight Night on September 4|url=https://mma.uno/alex-perez-vs-matt-schnell-reprogramado-para-ufc-fight-night-del-4-de-septiembre/|access-date=2021-08-07|publisher=mma.uno}} {{in lang|es}} They were originally expected to meet at [[UFC 262]], before Perez was forced to pull out due to undisclosed reasons.{{Cite web|author=Farah Hannoun|date=2021-04-21|title=With Alex Perez out, Rogerio Bontorin targeted to fight Matt Schnell at UFC 262|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/04/ufc-262-alex-perez-out-rogerio-bontorin-to-fight-matt-schnell|access-date=2021-04-22|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com}} They were then booked again at [[UFC on ESPN: Barboza vs. Chikadze]], before being shifted to this event. However, the bout was yet again postponed for unknown reasons to this event.{{Cite web|author=Kristen King|date=2021-09-20|title=Fights on Tap: Brandon Moreno vs. Deiveson Figueiredo 3 among 15 UFC bouts announced|url=https://www.bloodyelbow.com/2021/9/20/22682209/fights-on-tap-brandon-moreno-vs-deiveson-figueiredo-3-among-15-ufc-fights-announced-mma-news|access-date=2021-09-29|publisher=bloodyelbow.com|archive-date=2022-05-10|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20220510215952/https://www.bloodyelbow.com/2021/9/20/22682209/fights-on-tap-brandon-moreno-vs-deiveson-figueiredo-3-among-15-ufc-fights-announced-mma-news|url-status=dead}} At the weigh-ins, Perez weighed in at {{convert|126.25|lb|kg}}, which was {{convert|0.25|lb|kg}} over the flyweight non-title fight limit. Shortly after, officials announced that the bout had been cancelled as Schnell was forced to withdraw due to a medical issue.{{Cite web|author=Alexander K. Lee|date=2021-12-10|title=UFC 269 weigh-in results: Charles Oliveira, Amanda Nunes, challengers on weight for title fights, one bout women's flyweight bout between [[Montana De La Rosa]] and [[Maycee Barber]] was expected to take place at the event.{{Cite web|date=2021-09-01|title=Montana De La Rosa vs. Maycee Barber added to UFC 269 on December 11|url=https://www.fightbookmma.com/report-montana-de-la-rosa-vs-maycee-barber-added-to-ufc-269-on-december-11th/|access-date=2021-09-01|author=Ross Markey|publisher=fightbookmma.com}} 269 fight with Maycee Barber|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2021/10/6/22712550/montana-de-la-rosa-out-of-ufc-269-fight-with-maycee-barber|access-date=2021-10-06|publisher=mmafighting.com}}{{Cite [[UFC Fight Night: Brunson vs. Till]] \n|followingevent= [[UFC 266|UFC 266: Volkanovski vs. Ortega]]\n}}\n\n'''''UFC Fight Night: Smith vs. Spann''''' (also known as '''''UFC Fight Night 192''''', '''''UFC Vegas 37''''' and '''''UFC on ESPN+ 50''''') was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that took place on September 18, 2021, at the [[UFC Apex]] facility in [[Enterprise, Nevada]], part of the [[Las Vegas–Paradise, NV MSA|Las Vegas Metropolitan Area]], United States.{{Cite web|title=BREAKING: Dakota Bush vs. Rongzhu added to UFC event on September 18|url=https://mmadna.nl/breaking-dakota-bush-vs-rongzhu-toegevoegd-aan-ufc-evenement-op-18-september/|publisher=mmadna.nl|author=Marcel Dorff|date=2021-05-17|access-date=2021-05-26}} {{in lang|nl}}\n\n==Background==\nA light heavyweight bout between former [[UFC Light Heavyweight Championship]] title challenger [[Anthony Smith (fighter)|Anthony Smith]] and [[Ryan Spann]] served as the main event.{{cite web|url=https://www.bloodyelbow.com/2021/6/9/22524736/ufc-anthony-smith-vs-ryan-spann-headline-september-event-light-heavyweight-mma-news|title=Anthony Smith vs. Ryan Spann set to headline September UFC event|publisher=bloodyelbow.com|author=Tim Burke|date=2021-09-09|access-date=2021-08-24|archive-date=2021-08-13|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20210813150809/https://www.bloodyelbow.com/2021/6/9/22524736/ufc-anthony-smith-vs-ryan-spann-headline-september-event-light-heavyweight-mma-news|url-status=dead}}\n\nA light heavyweight bout between [[Ion Cuțelaba]] and [[Devin Clark (fighter)|Devin Clark]] was rescheduled to take place at this event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2021/6/16/22536183/ion-cutelaba-vs-devin-clark-booked-for-upcoming-ufc-event-on-sept-18|title=Ion Cutelaba vs. Devin Clark booked for upcoming UFC event on Sept. 18|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Damon Martin and Mike Heck|date=2021-06-16|access-date=2021-06-16}} The bout was previously scheduled to take place in May at [[UFC on ESPN: Reyes vs. Procházka]], but Clark pulled out of that event citing injury.{{Cite web|url=https://cagesidepress.com/2021/04/20/devin-clark-ion-cutelaba-now-faces-dustin-jacoby/|title=With Devin Clark out, Ion Cutelaba now faces Dustin Jacoby at UFC Vegas 25|publisher=cagesidepress.com|author=Jay Anderson|date=2021-04-20|access-date=2021-04-22}}\n\nA bantamweight bout between [[Tony Gravely]] and [[Nate Maness]] took place at this event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmadna.nl/tweede-poging-tony-gravely-vs-nate-maness-ditmaal-op-18-september/|title=Second try: Tony Gravely vs. Nate Maness, this time on September 18th|publisher=mmadna.nl|author=Marcel Dorff|date=2021-08-17|access-date=2021-08-17}} {{in lang|nl}} The pairing was previously scheduled to meet at [[UFC on ESPN: Whittaker vs. Gastelum]], but it was scrapped after Maness was removed from the bout for undisclosed reasons.{{Cite web|url=https://mmadna.nl/anthony-birchak-vervangt-nate-maness-tegen-tony-gravely-tijdens-ufc-evenement-op-17-april/|title=Anthony Birchak replaces Nate Maness against Tony Gravely at UFC event on April 17th|publisher=mmadna.nl|author=Marcel Dorff|date=2021-03-26|access-date=2021-03-26}} {{in lang|nl}}\n\nA bantamweight bout between [[Montel Jackson]] and [[Danaa Batgerel]] was scheduled for the event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmadna.nl/breaking-montel-jackson-treft-danaa-batgerel-op-18-september/|title=Montel Jackson meets Danaa Batgerel on September 18|publisher=mmadna.nl|author=Jamal Boussenaf|date=2021-06-03|access-date=2021-06-03}} {{in lang|nl}} However, Batgerel was pulled from the event due to [[Travel visa|visa]] issues and replaced by JP Buys.{{Cite web|url=https://mmadna.nl/breaking-jp-buys-accepteert-short-notice-partij-tegen-montel-jackson-op-18-september/|title=BREAKING: JP Buys accepts short notice game against Montel Jackson on September 18|publisher=mmadna.nl|author=Marcel Dorff|date=2021-08-20|access-date=2021-08-20}} {{in lang|nl}}\n\n[[Cory McKenna]] was expected to face [[Emily Whitmire]] in a women's strawweight bout at this event.{{cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/07/ufc-news-cory-mckenna-vs-emily-whitmire-strawweight-fight-night-sept-18|title=Cory McKenna vs. Emily Whitmire added to UFC Fight Night on Goldy]].{{cite web|url=https://www.marseillenews.net/mckenna-absent-hannah-goldy-affronte-emily-whitmire-a-lufc-fight-night-le-18-septembre.html|title=McKenna absent, Hannah Goldy faces Emily Whitmire at UFC Fight Night on September 18|publisher=marseillenews.net|author=Staff|date=2021-08-23|access-date=2021-08-24}} {{in lang|fr}}\n\nA women's flyweight bout between former [[Konfrontacja Sztuk [[UFC on ESPN: Kattar vs. Chikadze]]\n}}\n\n'''''UFC Fight Night: Lewis vs. Daukaus''''' (also known as '''''UFC Fight Night 199''''', '''''UFC on ESPN+ 57''''' and '''''UFC Vegas 45''''') was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that took place on December 18, 2021 at the [[UFC Apex]] facility in [[Enterprise, Nevada]], part of the [[Las Vegas–Paradise, NV MSA|Las Vegas Metropolitan Area]], United States.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/lists/mma-news-ufc-269-dustin-poirier-vs-charles-oliveira-las-vegas-nsac-approved|title=NAC approves UFC 269, two other UFC events for December in Las Vegas|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2021-10-20|access-date=2021-10-20}}\n\n==Background==\nA heavyweight bout between former [[UFC Heavyweight Championship]] challenger [[Derrick Lewis]] and [[Chris Daukaus]] headlined the event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.bjpenn.com/mma-news/ufc/derrick-lewis-returns-in-december-against-rising-contender-chris-daukaus/|title=Derrick Lewis returns in December against rising contender Chris Daukaus|publisher=bjpenn.com|author=Cole Shelton|date=2021-09-28|access-date=2021-09-28}}\n\nA women's strawweight bout between [[Amanda Lemos]] and [[Nina Nunes]] was scheduled for the event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/08/amanda-lemos-vs-nina-nunes-ansaroff-december-18-ufc-fight-booking-women-mma|title=UFC adds Amanda Lemos vs. Nina Nunes to Dec. 18 event|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Farah Hannoun|date=2021-08-19|access-date=2021-08-20}} However, Nunes was removed from the bout due to undisclosed reasons and was replaced by former [[Invicta Fighting Championships#Strawweight Championship|Invicta FC Strawweight Champion]] [[Angela Hill]].{{Cite web|date=2021-11-09|title=Angela Hill replaces Nina Nunes against Amanda Lemos on December 18 at UFC FN 199|url=https://mmadna.nl/angela-hill-vervangt-nina-nunes-tegen-amanda-lemos-op-18-december-tijdens-ufc-fn-199/|access-date=2021-11-10|publisher=mmadna.nl|author=Marcel Dorff}} {{in lang|nl}}\n\nA middleweight bout between [[Anthony Hernandez (fighter)|Anthony Hernandez]] and [[Dustin Stoltzfus]] was scheduled for the event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/09/ufc-fight-night-anthony-hernandez-vs-dustin-stoltzfus-in-works-dec-18|title=Anthony Hernandez vs. Dustin Stoltzfus in the works for UFC Fight Night on Dec. 18|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Farah Hannoun and Mike Bohn|date=2021-09-09|access-date=2021-09-09}} However, Hernandez withdrew from the event due to undisclosed reasons and was replaced by newcomer [[Caio Borralho]].{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2021/11/29/22808210/dustin-stoltzfus-caio-borralho-targeted-ufc-vegas-45-anthony-hernandez-injured|title=Dustin Stoltzfus vs. Caio Borralho targeted for UFC Vegas 45 with Anthony Hernandez injured|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Guilherme Cruz and Mike Heck|date=2021-11-29|access-date=2021-11-29}} In turn, Borralho and [[Gerald Meerschaert]]'s opponent, [[Abusupiyan Magomedov]], were forced to withdraw from the fight due to visa issues, thus the UFC matched Meerschaert and Stoltzfus against each other for the event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/12/ufc-fight-night-199-news-gerald-meerschaert-vs-dustin-stoltzfus-caio-borralho-abusupiyan-magomedov-out|title=Gerald Meerschaert vs. Dustin Stoltzfus in the works after two UFC Fight Night 199 drop-offs|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Nolan King|date=2021-12-03|access-date=2021-12-03}}\n\nA women's bantamweight bout between former [[UFC Women's Bantamweight Championship]] challenger [[Raquel Pennington]] and [[Julia Avila]] was scheduled for the event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmadna.nl/raquel-pennington-treft-julia-avila-tijdens-laatste-ufc-evenement-van-2021-op-18-december/|title=Raquel Pennington meets Julia Avila in final UFC event of 2021 on December [[Justin Tafa]] weighed in at 267 pounds, one pound over the heavyweight non-title fight limit (marking the first time in UFC history that a fighter missed weight in that division); [[Sijara Eubanks]] weighed in at 127.5 pounds, 1.5 pounds over the women's flyweight non-title fight limit; and Macy Chiasson weighed in at 148.5 pounds, 2.5 pounds over the women's featherweight non-title fight limit. All three bouts proceeded at a catchweight with Tafa, Eubanks and Chiasson each fined a percentage of their purses, which went to their opponents Harry Hunsucker, [[Melissa Gatto]] and [[Raquel Pennington]] respectively.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/12/ufc-fight-night-199-derrick-lewis-chris-daukaus-weigh-in-results-live-video-streamm|title=UFC Fight Night 199 weigh-in Lee|date=2021-12-03|access-date=2021-12-26}}\n\nA light heavyweight bout between [[Danilo Marques]] and [[Jailton Almeida]] was scheduled for [[UFC Fight Night: Holloway vs. Rodríguez]].{{Cite web|url= https://www.mmafighting.com/2021/9/17/22680219/dwcs-signee-jailton-almeida-november-ufc-debut-danilo-marques-mma|title=DWCS signee Jailton Almeida set for November UFC debut vs. Danilo Marques|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Guilherme Cruz|date=2021-09-17|access-date=2021-10-18}} However, the bout was rescheduled due to Marques getting injured, and was expected to take place at this event.{{Cite web|url=https://agfight.com/ufc/danilo-marques-se-lesiona-e-luta-com-malhadinho-no-ufc-e-adiada/|title=Danilo Marques is injured and fight with Malhadinho in the UFC is postponed|publisher=agfight.com|author=Diego Ribas|date=2021-10-07|access-date=2021-11-11}} {{in lang|pt}}\n\nA flyweight bout between [[Malcolm Gordon (fighter)|Malcolm Gordon]] and Denys Bondar was scheduled for [[UFC Fight Night: Vieira vs. Tate]].{{Cite web|url=https://www.ctvnews.ca/sports/article/canadas-malcolm-gordon-gavin-tucker-added-to-november-ufc-card-in-las-vegas/|title=Canada's Malcolm Gordon, Gavin Tucker added to November UFC card in Las Vegas|publisher=ctvnews.ca|author=The Canadian Press|date=2021-10-05|access-date=2021-11-12}} However, Gordon withdrew from the event due to undisclosed reasons, and the pairing was rescheduled for this date.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/11/ufc-fight-night-198-malcolm-gordon-out-vs-denys-bondar|title=Malcolm Gordon out of UFC Fight Night 198 bout; His bout proceeded at a catchweight and he was fined 30% of his purse, which went to his opponent [[Julian Erosa]].{{Cite web|title=UFC Fight Night 200 weigh-in results: One miss, but full lineup the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that took place on January 15, 2022 at the [[UFC Apex]] facility in [[Enterprise, Nevada]], part of the [[Las Vegas–Paradise, NV MSA|Las Vegas Metropolitan Area]], United States.{{Cite web|url=https://mmasucka.com/2021/12/08/ufc-applies-for-several-dates-at-the-apex-to-start-2022/|title=UFC applies for several dates at the Apex to start event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2021/10/27/22749847/calvin-kattar-vs-giga-chikadze-set-to-headline-jan-15-ufc-event|title=Calvin Kattar vs. Giga Chikadze set to headline Jan. 15 UFC 270|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Alexander K. Boussenaf|date=2021-10-19|access-date=2021-10-20}} {{in lang|nl}} Yoder eventually withdrew due to undisclosed reasons and was replaced by [[Silvana Gómez Juárez|Silvana Gomez Juarez]].{{Cite Gonzalez|date=2022-01-12|title=Vanessa Demopoulos vs. Silvana Juarez pushed back a week to UFC [[Joaquin Buckley]] and [[Abdul Razak Alhassan]] was scheduled for the event.{{Cite web|title=Previously scrapped Joaquin Buckley vs. Abdul Razak Alhassan bout rebooked for Feb. Wells|date=2022-01-11|access-date=2022-01-11}}\n\nA flyweight between Kleydson Rodrigues and [[Zarrukh Adashev]] was scheduled for the event.{{Cite web|publisher=sherdog.com|title=Kleydson Rodrigues injury.{{Cite web|author=Guilherme Cruz|title=Kleydson Rodrigues vs. Zarrukh Adashev pulled from UFC Vegas Brown]] and [[Gabriel Benitez]] was scheduled for the event.{{Cite web|title=Gabriel Benitez vs. TJ Brown added to UFC event on January 15|url=https://mmadna.nl/gabriel-benitez-vs-tj-brown-toegevoegd-aan-ufc-evenement-op-15-januari/|date=2021-12-06|access-date=2022-01-12|publisher=mmadna.nl|author=Marcel Dorff}} {{in lang|nl}} Three days before the event, Benitez was forced to pull out and [[Charles Rosa]] replaced him, therefore changing the contest to the lightweight division.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2022/1/12/22879930/gabriel-benitez-out-charles-rosa-steps-in-on-3-days-notice-to-fight-tj-brown-at-ufc-vegas-46|title=Gabriel Benitez out, Charles Rosa steps in on 3 days’ notice to fight TJ Brown at UFC Vegas 46|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Alexander K. Kelleher vs. Saidyokub Kakhramonov set for January 15 UFC event|url=https://cagesidepress.com/2021/10/02/brian-kelleher-vs-saidyokub-kakhramonov-set-for-january-15-ufc-event/|access-date=2022-01-12|publisher=cagesidepress.com}} However, Kakhramonov withdrew
