272|UFC 272: Covington vs. Masvidal]]\n}}\n\n'''''UFC Fight Night: Makhachev vs. Green''''' (also known as '''''UFC Fight Night 202''''', '''''UFC on ESPN+ 60''''' and '''''UFC Vegas 49''''') was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that took place on February 26, 2022 at the [[UFC Apex]] facility in [[Enterprise, Nevada]], part of the [[Las Vegas–Paradise, NV MSA|Las Vegas Metropolitan Area]], United States.{{Cite web|url=https://mmasucka.com/2021/12/08/ufc-applies-for-several-dates-at-the-apex-to-start-2022/|title=UFC applies for several dates at the Apex to start 2022|publisher=mmasucka.com|author=Andrew Harbaugh|date=2021-12-08|access-date=2022-01-04}}\n\n==Background==\nA lightweight bout between [[Islam Makhachev]] and [[Beneil Dariush]] was expected to serve as the event headliner.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/11/ufc-fight-night-beneil-dariush-vs-islam-makhachev-booked-feb-26|title=Streaking Beneil Dariush, Islam Makhachev to headline UFC Fight Night on Feb. 26|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Simon Samano|date=2021-11-27|access-date=2021-11-27}} However on February 16, it was reported that Dariush withdrew from the event due to an ankle \ninjury and was replaced by [[King Green]] on just 10 days notice at a catchweight of 160 pounds.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2022/02/ufc-news-beneil-dariush-injured-out-main-event-vs-islam-makhachev-bobby-green-replacement|title=Beneil Dariush injured, Bobby Green meets Islam Makhachev in UFC Fight Night 202 main event|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Mike Bohn|date=2022-02-17|access-date=2022-02-17}}\n\nA lightweight bout between [[Farès Ziam]] and [[Terrance McKinney]] took place at the event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmadna.nl/terrance-mckinney-vs-fares-ziam-opnieuw-ingepland-ditmaal-voor-26-februari/|title=Terrance McKinney vs. Fares Ziam rescheduled, this time for February 26|publisher=mmadna.nl|author=Marcel Dorff|date=2021-12-03|access-date=2021-12-04}} {{in lang|nl}} The pair was previously booked for [[UFC Fight Night: Vieira vs. Tate]] in last November, but it was scrapped a few hours before taking place due to one of McKinney's cornermen [[COVID-19 testing|testing positive for COVID-19]].{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/11/ufc-fight-night-198-terrance-mckinney-vs-fares-ziam-canceled-covid-19-exposure|title=Terrance McKinney forced out of UFC Fight Night 198 due to COVID-19 exposure|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Simon Samano|date=2021-11-20|access-date=2021-11-20}}\n\nA heavyweight bout between [[Jairzinho Rozenstruik]] and [[Marcin Tybura]] was scheduled for the event.{{Cite web|author=Tristen Critchfield|date=2022-01-09|title=Heavyweights Jairzinho Rozenstruik, Marcin Tybura to meet at Feb. 26 UFC event|url=https://www.sherdog.com/news/news/Heavyweights-Jairzinho-Rozenstruik-Marcin-Tybura-to-Meet-at-Feb-26-UFC-Event-184057|access-date=2022-01-09|publisher=sherdog.com}} However in mid-January, the bout was moved to [[UFC 273]].{{Cite web|author=Jesse Holland|date=2022-01-13|title=Jairzinho Rozenstruik vs Marcin Tybura rebooked for UFC 273 on April 9|url=https://www.mmamania.com/2022/1/13/22882164/jairzinho-rozenstruik-vs-marcin-tybura-rebooked-ufc-273-april-9-mma|access-date=2022-01-14|publisher=mmamania.com}}\n\nA women's bantamweight bout between [[Wu Yanan (fighter)|Wu Yanan]] and [[Josiane Nunes]] was scheduled for the event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.eurosport.nl/mixed-martial-arts/performance-bonus-winnaar-nunes-nu-tegen-yanan-tijdens-ufc-vegas-49_sto8708610/story.shtml|title=Performance bonus winner Nunes now against Yanan at UFC Vegas 49|publisher=eurosport.nl|author=Marcel Dorff|date=2022-01-13|access-date=2022-01-13}} {{in lang|nl}} However, Wu was pulled from the fight for undisclosed reasons and she was replaced by promotional newcomer Jennifer Gonzalez.{{Cite web|url=https://www.eurosport.nl/mixed-martial-arts/ufc/2022/gonzalez-maakt-ufc-debuut-tegen-nunes-tijdens-ufc-vegas-49_sto8739546/story.shtml|title=Gonzalez makes UFC debut against Nunes at UFC Vegas 49|publisher=eurosport.nl|author=Marcel Dorff|date=2022-01-26|access-date=2022-01-27}} {{in lang|nl}} In turn, Gonzalez was released from UFC one week ahead of the event for undisclosed reasons (later revealed to be related to an [[USADA]] incident).{{Cite web|author=Alexander K. web|url=https://www.cbssports.com/mma/news/ufc-243-results-highlights-israel-adesanya-claims-middleweight-crown-with-violent-tko-of-robert-whittaker/|title=UFC 243 results, highlights: Israel Adesanya 12|publisher=mmanews.com|author=Clyde Aidoo|date=2021-12-15|access-date=2021-12-15}} They were previously scheduled to meet on four occasions ([[UFC 262]], [[UFC on ESPN: Barboza vs. Chikadze]], [[UFC Ankalaev]] served as the event headliner.{{Cite web|url=https://mmadna.nl/thiago-santos-vs-magomed-ankalaev-is-het-main-event-tijdens-ufc-evenement-op-12-maart/|title=Thiago Santos vs. Magomed '''''UFC Fight Night 204''''' and '''''UFC on ESPN+ 62''''') was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that took place on March 19, 2022, at [[The O2 Arena|The O2 Arena]] in [[London]], England.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2022/01/ufc-officially-announces-return-to-london-england-for-march-19-event|title=UFC officially announces return to London for March 19 event|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Farah Hannoun|date=2022-01-19|access-date=2022-01-19|archive-date=4 February 2022|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20220204010308/https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2022/01/ufc-officially-announces-return-to-london-england-for-march-19-event|url-status=live}}\n\n==Background==\nThe event was the first in [[London]] since [[UFC Fight Night: Till vs. Masvidal]] in March 2019. The UFC was expected to return to the city in March 2020 at [[UFC Fight Night: Woodley vs. Edwards]] and in September 2021 at [[UFC Fight Night: Brunson vs. Till]], but the first event was cancelled due to the [[COVID-19 pandemic]], and the latter was relocated to [[Las Vegas]].\n\nA heavyweight bout between former [[List of Bellator MMA champions#Heavyweight World Championship|Bellator Heavyweight World Champion]] [[Alexander Volkov (fighter)|Alexander Volkov]] and [[Tom Aspinall]] headlined the event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2022/01/ufc-fight-night-london-alexander-volkov-vs-tom-aspinall-march-19-mma-matchup-booking-announcement|title=Alexander Volkov vs. Tom Aspinall headlines UFC's March return to London|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|date=2022-01-20|access-date=2022-01-21|archive-date=19 April 2022|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20220419134943/https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2022/01/ufc-fight-night-london-alexander-volkov-vs-tom-aspinall-march-19-mma-matchup-booking-announcement|url-status=live}} Aspinall was originally expected to face [[Shamil Abdurakhimov]], but after he was pulled in favor of the main event spot, Abdurakhimov was rescheduled against [[Sergei Pavlovich]] instead.{{Cite web|url=https://cagesidepress.com/2021/11/02/tom-aspinall-vs-shamil-abdurakhimov-set-march-19-ufc-event/|title=Tom Aspinall vs. Shamil Abdurakhimov set for March 19 UFC Green]]\n|followingevent= [[UFC Fight Night: Santos vs. Ankalaev]]\n}}\n\n'''''UFC 272: Covington vs. Masvidal''''' was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that took place on March 5, 2022, at the [[T-Mobile Arena]] in [[Paradise, Nevada|Paradise]], [[Nevada]], part of the [[Las Vegas–Paradise, NV MSA|Las Vegas Metropolitan Area]], United States.{{Cite web|url=https://www.espn.com/mma/story/_/id/33001503/ufc-272-feature-alexander-volkanovski-max-holloway-aljamain-sterling-petr-yan-fights|title=UFC 272 to feature Alexander Volkanovski-Max Holloway, Aljamain Sterling-Petr Yan fights|work=espn.com|author=Brett Okamoto|date=2022-01-05|access-date=2022-01-05}}\n\n==Background==\nA [[UFC Featherweight Championship]] trilogy bout between current champion [[Alexander Volkanovski]] and former champion [[Max Holloway]] was originally expected to headline the event.{{Cite web|author=Damon Martin|date=2022-01-05|title=Alexander Volkanovski vs. Max Holloway 3, Aljamain Sterling vs. Petr Yan 2 booked for UFC 272|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2022/1/5/22869471/alexander-volkanovski-vs-max-holloway-3-aljamain-sterling-vs-petr-yan-2-booked-for-ufc-272|access-date=2022-01-06|publisher=mmafighting.com}} The pairing first met at [[UFC 245]], where Volkanovski defeated Holloway via unanimous decision to capture the title.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2019/12/ufc-245-results-alexander-volkanovski-dethrones-max-holloway-captures-ufc-featherweight-belt|title=UFC 245 results: Alexander Volkanovski dethrones Max Holloway, captures UFC featherweight belt|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Danny Segura|date=2019-12-15|access-date=2022-01-05}} Their second meeting took place at [[UFC 251]], with Volkanovski successfully defending the title via split decision (a result which was met with controversy by fans, fellow fighters, and media outlets alike).{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2020/7/12/21321539/ufc-251-results-alexander-volkanovski-retains-title-razor-close-split-decision-over-max-holloway|title=UFC 251 results: Alexander Volkanovski retains title with razor-close split decision over Max Holloway|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2020-07-12|access-date=2022-01-05}}{{Cite web|date=2020-07-12|title=Twitter reacts to Volkanovski-Holloway 2 decision|url=https://www.espnhonolulu.com/2020/07/12/volkanovski-holloway-2-decision/|access-date=2022-01-06|publisher=espnhonolulu.com|author=Staff|archive-date=2022-01-06|archive-url=https://web.archive.org/web/20220106112740/https://www.espnhonolulu.com/2020/07/12/volkanovski-holloway-2-decision/|url-status=dead}} However, two days after the trilogy announcement, Holloway withdrew due to an aggravated previous injury.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2022/01/ufc-272-max-holloway-injured-out-vs-alexander-volkanovski-trilogy-title-fight|title=Max Holloway injury takes him out of UFC 272 trilogy fight vs. Alexander Volkanovski|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Staff|date=2022-01-07|access-date=2022-01-07}} He was replaced by former title challenger [[Chan Sung Jung]] and the bout was moved to [[UFC 273]] on April 9.{{cite web|url=https://www.cbssports.com/mma/news/ufc-273-fight-card-alexander-volkanovski-vs-korean-zombie-aljamain-sterling-vs-petr-yan-2-set-for-april/|title=UFC 273 fight card: Alexander Volkanovski vs. Korean Zombie, Aljamain Sterling vs. Petr Yan 2 set for April|publisher=cbssports.com|author=Shakiel Mahjouri and Brent Brookhouse|date=2022-01-11|access-date=2022-01-11}}\n\nA [[UFC Bantamweight Championship]] title unification rematch between current champion [[Aljamain Sterling]] and former champion/current interim title holder [[Petr Yan]] was expected to take place at this event. The pairing previously met at [[UFC 259]], with Sterling winning the title by disqualification (intentional illegal knee strike) in the fourth round, becoming the first fighter to win a UFC title by disqualification.{{Cite web|url=https://www.cbssports.com/mma/news/ufc-259-results-highlights-aljamain-sterling-earns-135-pound-title-after-petr-yans-illegal-knee-leads-to-dq/|title=UFC 259 results: Aljamain Sterling earns 135-pound title after Petr Yan's illegal knee leads to DQ|publisher=cbssports.com|author=Dan Doherty|date=2021-03-07|access-date=2022-01-05}} The rematch was initially expected to take place at [[UFC 267]], however Sterling withdrew from the contest due to lingering neck issues and an interim title bout between Yan and [[Cory Sandhagen]] was set.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2021/9/25/22693315/aljamain-sterling-out-of-ufc-267-title-fight-vs-petr-yan|title=Aljamain Sterling out of UFC 267 title fight vs. Petr Yan|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Mike Heck and Damon Martin|date=2021-09-25|access-date=2021-01-05}} The UFC then opted to move the pairing from this event to [[UFC 273]].{{Cite web|url= https://www.mmafighting.com/2022/1/11/22872809/alexander-volkanovski-vs-the-korean-zombie-aljamain-sterling-vs-petr-yan-2-set-to-headline-ufc-273|title=Alexander Volkanovski vs. The Korean Zombie, Aljamain Sterling vs. Petr Yan 2 set to headline UFC 273|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Mike Heck|date=2021-01-11|access-date=2021-01-11}}\n\nAfter the postponement of the title fights, a welterweight bout between former interim [[UFC Welterweight Championship|UFC Welterweight Champion]] [[Colby Covington]] and former title challenger [[Jorge Masvidal]] headlined the event.{{Cite web|url= https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2022/01/ufc-272-colby-covington-vs-jorge-masvidal-headliner-march-5|title= Colby Covington vs. Jorge Masvidal to headline UFC 272 on March 5|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Danny Segura|date=2022-01-11|access-date=2022-01-11}}\n\nA heavyweight bout between [[Greg Hardy]] and [[Serghei Spivac]] was originally scheduled for [[UFC 270]], but it was moved to this event after Hardy suffered a finger injury.{{Cite web|date=2022-01-19|title=Hardy vs. Spivac moves to UFC 272 in Las Vegas|url=https://www.eurosport.nl/mixed-martial-arts/ufc/2022/hardy-vs.-spivac-verplaatst-naar-ufc-272-in-las-vegas_sto8720759/story.shtml|access-date=2022-01-19|publisher=eurosport.nl|author=Marcel Dorff}} {{in lang|nl}}\n\nA five round lightweight bout between former [[UFC Lightweight Championship|UFC Lightweight Champion]] [[Rafael dos Anjos]] and [[Rafael Fiziev]] was originally scheduled to headline [[UFC Fight Night: Walker vs. Hill]], but it was postponed to this event due to visa issues with Fiziev.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmamania.com/2022/2/11/22929558/rafael-dos-anjos-vs-rafael-fiziev-off-ufc-vegas-48-rebooked-for-ufc-272-co-main-event-mma|title=Rafael dos Anjos vs Rafael Fiziev off UFC Vegas 48, positive for COVID-19]].{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2022/2/28/22955195/rafael-fiziev-out-of-ufc-272-fight-against-rafael-dos-anjos-due-to-covid-19 |title=Rafael Fiziev out of UFC 272 Vegas 48''''') was a [[mixed martial arts]] event produced by the [[Ultimate Fighting Championship]] that took place on February 19, 2022, at the [[UFC Apex]] facility in [[Enterprise, Nevada]], part of the [[Las Vegas–Paradise, NV MSA|Las Vegas Metropolitan Area]], United States.{{Cite web|url=https://mmasucka.com/2021/12/08/ufc-applies-for-several-dates-at-the-apex-to-start-2022/|title=UFC applies for several dates at the Apex to start 2022|publisher=mmasucka.com|author=Andrew Harbaugh|date=2021-12-08|access-date=2022-01-04}}\n\n==Background==\nA lightweight bout between former [[UFC Lightweight Championship|UFC Lightweight Champion]] [[Rafael dos Anjos]] and [[Rafael Fiziev]] was expected to serve as the event headliner.{{Cite web|url=https://www.sherdog.com/news/news/Rafael-dos-Anjos-vs-Rafael-Fiziev-Headlines-Feb-19-UFC-Fight-Night-Card-184034|title=Rafael dos Anjos vs. Rafael Fiziev headlines Feb. 19 UFC Fight Night card|publisher=sherdog.com|author=Jay Pettry|date=2022-01-07|access-date=2022-01-07}} However, the bout was postponed to [[UFC 272]] due to visa issues with Fiziev. Instead, a light heavyweight bout between [[Johnny Walker (fighter)|Johnny Walker]] and [[Jamahal Hill]] served as the main event.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmamania.com/2022/2/11/22929558/rafael-dos-anjos-vs-rafael-fiziev-off-ufc-vegas-48-rebooked-for-ufc-272-co-main-event-mma|title=Rafael dos Anjos vs Rafael Fiziev off UFC Vegas 48, rebooked for UFC 272 co-main event|publisher=mmamania.com|author=Jesse Holland|date=2022-02-11|access-date=2022-02-11}}\n\n[[Jim Miller (fighter)|Jim Miller]] and Nikolas Motta met in a lightweight bout at this event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2022/01/ufc-jim-miller-vs-nikolas-motta-rebooked-february-19-mma-fight-booking-matchup|title=Jim Miller vs. Nikolas Motta rebooked for February after 2021 cancellation|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Staff|date=2021-12-08|access-date=2022-01-04}} They were originally booked for [[UFC Fight Night: Smith vs. Spann]] in last September, but Miller [[COVID-19 testing|tested positive for COVID-19]] a week before that event and was pulled from the contest.{{Cite web|url= https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/09/jim-miller-out-covid-19-coronavirus-vs-nikolas-motta-ufc-fight-night-192|title=Jim Miller says COVID-19 has him out of UFC Fight Night 192 vs. Nikolas Motta|publisher=mmajunkie.usatoday.com|author=Staff|date=2021-09-10|access-date=2021-09-10}}\n\n[[John Makdessi]] was expected to face [[Nasrat Haqparast]] in a lightweight bout.{{Cite web|url=https://www.mmafighting.com/2021/11/15/22783664/nasrat-haqparast-vs-john-makdessi-booked-for-ufc-event-on-feb-19|title=Nasrat Haqparast vs. John Makdessi booked for UFC event on Feb. 19|publisher=mmafighting.com|author=Damon Martin|date=2021-11-15|access-date=2022-02-18}} However, Makdessi pulled out due to injury and the pairing was scrapped.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2022/01/ufc-271-news-bobby-green-vs-nasrat-haqparast-added|title=Bobby Green vs. Nasrat Haqparast booked for UFC (fighter)|Alexander Romanov]] was expected to take place at the event.{{Cite web|url=https://cagesidepress.com/2021/12/28/ilir-latifi-vs-alexander-romanov-announced-february-19/|title=UFC: Ilir Latifi vs. Alexander Romanov announced for February 19|publisher=cagesidepress.com|author=Jay Anderson|date=2021-12-28|access-date=2021-02-05}} However, Romanov withdrew due to injury and the bout was Columbus|publisher=bjpenn.com|author=Cole Shelton|date=2022-02-04|access-date=2021-02-05}}\n\nA middleweight bout featuring [[Julian Marquez]] and [[Kyle Daukaus]] was scheduled for the event.{{Cite web|url=https://mmajunkie.usatoday.com/2021/11/kyle-daukaus-vs-julian-marquez-february-19-ufc-mma-fight-booking-matchup-announcement|title=UFC targets Kyle Daukaus vs. Julian Marquez for Feb. 19
